## Independent lead probe: P-8 default spoof probe
- canary verify (no profile, unnamed public override) exit=1
- printed spoof line:   spoof probe (--with-spoof-probe): disabled: forwarding claims are shown and compared, but only HDD response headers can make them evidence (P-7/P-8)
- target refusal: CANARY-TARGET    FAIL  --through-url: --through-url `https://third-party.example` is refused before any request: no live profile is loaded, so an override is only allowed with --local-only and a loopback literal
- help flag:       --with-spoof-probe           Plant a bogus forwarding token in the echo probe (default off). HDD strips it and rebuilds the chain; without this flag the forwarding claim is shown and compared but never asserted as HDD evidence (P-8)
